## Loot Calibration

**Treasure Calibration Table**

| Challenge Rating (CR) | Bireysel Hazine (Individual) | Hazine Sandığı (Treasure Hoard) | Önerilen Eşya / Hazine Haritası |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **CR 0 – 4** *(East Blue)* | `3d6 x ฿10,000` *(Ort. ฿100,000)* | `2d4 x ฿1,000,000` *(Ort. ฿5,000,000)* | `1d4 - 1` Simple Dial veya CR 0–4 Hazine Haritası |
| **CR 5 – 10** *(Paradise)* | `2d8 x ฿100,000` *(Ort. ฿900,000)* | `8d10 x ฿1,000,000` *(Ort. ฿44,000,000)* | `1d3` Exotic Dial, Pop Greens veya CR 5–10 Hazine Haritası |
| **CR 11 – 16** *(New World)* | `2d10 x ฿1,000,000` *(Ort. ฿11,000,000)* | `8d8 x ฿10,000,000` *(Ort. ฿360,000,000)* | `1d4` Rare Eşya, Adam Wood kütüğü veya CR 11–16 Haritası |
| **CR 17+** *(Yonko & Amiraller)* | `2d8 x ฿10,000,000` *(Ort. ฿90,000,000)* | `6d10 x ฿100,000,000` *(Ort. ฿3.3 Milyar)* | `1d6` Legendary Eşya, Şeytan Meyvesi veya Poneglyph Rubbing |

**Tablo Sütunları ve Kullanım Rehberi:**
- **Challenge Rating (CR):** Karşılaşmanın zorluğunu ve temsil ettiği One Piece deniz kademesini belirtir.
- **Bireysel Hazine (Individual):** Mağlup edilen tekil bir düşmanın üzerinden (ceplerinden veya kişisel çantasından) çıkan doğrudan nakittir. Küçük çatışmalarda her düşman için bu zar atılır.
- **Hazine Sandığı (Treasure Hoard):** Düşman üssü, korsan gemisi zulası veya boss inindeki ana kasadan çıkan toplam nakit paradır.
- **Önerilen Eşya / Hazine Haritası:** Sandıkta nakde ek olarak sunulacak tematik eşyaları, hammaddeleri veya yeni bir macera başlatan Hazine Haritasını gösterir.

DMG 2024 Chapter 4 tablolarındaki `1 GP = ฿10,000` doğrudan çevrimi yüksek CR'larda kontrolsüz enflasyon yaratır. Bu tablo nakit miktarını makul seviyede tutmak için tasarlanmıştır; ganimetin kalan değerini nakit yerine Hazine Haritası, gemi modifikasyonları veya Şeytan Meyvesi olarak verin.

## Treasure Maps

Korsanlar servetlerini açık denizde taşımak yerine ıssız adalara gömerler. Hazine haritaları, DM'in sunduğu birer One-Shot veya Short Campaign macera kancasıdır.

Haritanın ulaştırdığı ganimet yukarıdaki Hazine Sandığı (Treasure Hoard) tablosu kullanılarak hesaplanır. DM, yolculuk esnasında aşağıdaki komplikasyon tablosunu kullanabilir:

**Treasure Map Complications (1d10)**

| `1d10` | Komplikasyon | Tematik Sonuç & Macera Durumu |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| **1** | **Yağmalanmış Kasa** | Zula başka bir korsan tarafından daha önce açılmıştır; sandıkta alaycı bir not veya rakip bir korsanın Wanted posteri bırakılmıştır. |
| **2** | **Canavar Yuvası** | Sandığın gömülü olduğu mağara veya yarık, devasa bir Apex Predator veya antik bir ada canavarının inidir; kazı anında canavar uyanır. |
| **3** | **Pusu Kuran Rakipler** | Haritanın kopyasını veya dedikodusunu takip eden rakip bir korsan müfrezesi ya da ödül avcıları kazı anında pusu kurar. |
| **4** | **Mistik Koruma Laneti** | Sandık açıldığında çevreye kadim bir lanet veya dehşet aurası yayılır; sandığı açanlar mistik bir korku veya irade sınavıyla yüzleşir. |
| **5** | **Çifte Gizli Bölme** | Sandığın sahte bir tabanı vardır; dikkatli bir araştırma sonucunda gizli bölmede özel ödüller keşfedilir. |
| **6** | **Marine Devriyesi / Ablukası** | Kazı bölgesine veya ada kıyısına bir Marine devriye gemisi yanaşmıştır; gizlice kaçmak ya da abluka hattını yarmak gerekir. |
| **7** | **Ada Sakinlerinin Kutsal Sunağı** | Sandık, yerli bir kabile veya keşişler tarafından dini bir sunak kabul edilmiştir; hazineyi almak bir kriz doğurur. |
| **8** | **Geçici İttifak Teklifi** | Hazine başında karşılaşan rakip bir korsan kaptanı, hemen savaşmak yerine ganimeti paylaşmayı veya adadaki ölümcül bir tehlikeye karşı birlik olmayı teklif eder. |
| **9** | **Vivre Card ile Takip Edilen Sandık** | Hazine sandığına iliştirilmiş bir Vivre Card parçası vardır; sandık açıldığı an asıl sahibi sandığın hareket ettiğini anlayıp yola çıkmıştır. |
| **10** | **Tehlikeli Coğrafi Konum** | Sandık aktif bir volkanik bacanın kenarında, batmakta olan bir enkazda veya uçurumdan sarkan köklerin ucundadır; çevre manipülasyonu ve kurtarma planı gerektirir. |

## Setting Items Catalog

Aşağıdaki liste, diğer bölümlerde bulunmayan One Piece evrenine özgü nadir teknolojileri, cephaneleri ve prototipleri içerir:

- **Raid Suit (Germa 66 Teknolojisi):** Kompakt metal silindir kutularda taşınan, giyen kişiye uçuş kabiliyeti, artırılmış zırh koruması ve modeline göre ateş, elektrik veya görünmezlik gibi özel teknolojik güçler bahşeden biyomühendislik ürünü giyilebilir zırhlar.
- **Excite Bullets (Veba Mermileri):** Queen ve Beasts Pirates tarafından geliştirilen, isabet alan kurbanların bedenine Mummy (mumyalaştırıcı çürüme) veya Ice Oni (kontrolsüz saldırganlık ve dondurucu buz pulları) gibi ölümcül kimyasal virüsler bulaştıran özel cephaneler.
- **Energy Steroids (Enerji Hapları):** Balıkadam Adası yeraltından çıkan, tüketildiğinde geçici olarak kullanıcının kas gücünü insanüstü seviyelere fırlatan ancak bedeli hızlandırılmış yaşlanma ve kalıcı hayati yıpranma olan yasaklı kimyasallar.
- **Rumble Ball (Zoan Modifikasyon Hapı):** Zoan türü Şeytan Meyvesi dalga boylarını manipüle ederek kullanıcıya standart formların ötesinde fazladan dönüşüm noktaları (Guard Point, Horn Point vb.) kazandıran tıbbi haplar. Kısa sürede fazla tüketilmesi kontrol kaybına ve kontrolsüz Monster Point formuna yol açar.
- **Muggy Ball / Özel Minyatür Patlayıcılar:** Barut uzmanları ve mühendisler tarafından küçük bilyeler halinde üretilen, cebe sığacak boyutta olmasına rağmen patladığında devasa bir top mermisinin tahribatını ve alev dalgasını yaratan özel patlayıcılar.
- **Deniz Taşı (Seastone) Silahları ve Teçhizatı:** Deniz enerjisini barındıran ve Şeytan Meyvesi kullanıcılarını etkisiz kılan mineral. Anlık Vuruş (meyve savunmalarını ve Logia dokunulmazlığını aşma) ve Sürekli Temas (Powerless condition) mekanikleri ile Seastone kelepçe, silah ve mühimmat kuralları için [Chapter 5: Equipment](#seastone-equipment-and-mechanics) bölümüne bakın.
- **Egghead Fütüristik İcatları (Dr. Vegapunk Teknolojileri):** Dikey yüzeylerde yürümeyi sağlayan DOM Shoes, meyve kullanıcılarını yakalayan Bubble Gun, lazerleri elle tutmayı sağlayan Light-Pressure Gloves ve yapay Paramecia faktörü taşıyan Green Blood gibi dünyanın 500 yıl ilerisindeki prototipler.

## Devil Fruits as Treasure

Şeytan Meyveleri açık denizdeki en değerli hazinelerdir. Rastgele ganimet sandıklarında Şeytan Meyvesi çıkma olasılığı ve karaborsa değerleri aşağıdadır:

**Devil Fruit Hoard Chance (d100)**

| Bölge / CR Kademesi | Meyve Çıkma İhtimali (`d100`) | Olası Rarity Kademesi |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **Four Blues (CR 0–4)** | %1 – %5 (96–100) | Uncommon (Basit Paramecia veya Zoan) |
| **Grand Line / Paradise (CR 5–10)** | %1 – %15 (86–100) | Uncommon (%70) veya Rare (%30) |
| **New World (CR 11–16)** | %1 – %30 (71–100) | Rare (%60), Very Rare (%35), Legendary (%5) |
| **Yonko / Hükümet Kasası (CR 17+)** | %1 – %60 (41–100) | Very Rare (%60) veya Legendary (%40) |

**Ganimet Olarak Meyve Çıktığında:**
1. **Meyve Türü ve Belirleme:** Sandıktan çıkan meyvenin Rarity derecesi yukarıdaki tablodan belirlendikten sonra, hangi spesifik meyvenin bulunduğunu rastgele tespit etmek için [Devil Fruit Generation](#devil-fruit-generation) altındaki ilgili tablodan zar atın (`d97`, `d93`, `d28` veya `d22`).
2. **Tanımlama ve Gizli Kimlik:** Oyuncular meyvenin adını ve gücünü hemen bilemezler. Meyvenin gizli kimliğini korumak için [Gizli Meyve Kimliği (Fruit ID)](#devil-fruit-identification) numarasını kullanın; oyuncuların meyveyi bilim insanlarına veya karaborsa sarraflarına inceletme kuralları için [Devil Fruit Identification](#devil-fruit-identification) bölümüne başvurun.
3. **Mekanikler ve İstatistikler:** Bulunan meyvenin tüm kuralları, istatistikleri, büyüleri ve dönüşüm formları için [Chapter 7: Devil Fruits](#chapter-7-devil-fruits) bölümündeki ilgili sınıf rehberine başvurun.

### Black Market Pricing

* **Tanımlanamamış / Şekli Bilinmeyen Meyve:** Taban ฿100,000,000.
* **Uncommon Şeytan Meyvesi:** ฿100,000,000 – ฿250,000,000.
* **Rare Şeytan Meyvesi:** ฿250,000,000 – ฿750,000,000.
* **Very Rare Şeytan Meyvesi:** ฿750,000,000 – ฿2,000,000,000.
* **Legendary Şeytan Meyvesi (Ope Ope no Mi vb.):** ฿2,000,000,000 – ฿5,000,000,000+.

> Karaborsa uzmanlarının güvenilirlik oranları, aldatmaca riskleri ve değerleme DC'leri için [Devil Fruit Identification](#devil-fruit-identification) kurallarına bakın.

# Chapter 14: Bestiary and Monster Engine

One Piece dünyasında tehlike yalnızca korsan kaptanları veya Marine amirallerinden ibaret değildir; Calm Belt'in titanik Sea King'leri, Grand Line adalarının tarih öncesi megafaunası, World Government'ın sibernetik silahları ve kadim devleri bu evrenin yaşayan ekosistemini oluşturur. Bu bölüm; Monster Manual yaratıklarını One Piece evrenine uyarlayan Reskin eşleşmelerini, 554 canavardan derlenmiş Modüler Yetenek Kütüphanesi'ni, CR kademelendirmeli 7 Arketipik Canavar Şablonu'nu, efsanevi tekil varlıklar fihristini ve özel eğitilmiş NotebookLM canavar motoru bağlantısını sunar.

- **Reskin Reference Library:** Monster Manual yaratıklarının doğrudan One Piece evrenine uyarlandığı hızlı referans listesi.
- **Modular Monster Feature Library:** Farklı sourcebook'lardaki 554 resmi yaratıktan filtrelenmiş 5 modüler yetenek tablosu (Traits, Actions, Bonus Actions, Reactions, Legendary Actions).
- **Archetypal Monster Templates:** Evrenin 7 ikonik grubu için modüler monster sheet ve CR kademelendirme tabloları.
- **Unique & Mythological Creatures:** Mitolojik deniz varlıkları, World Government antik tehditleri ve efsanevi canavarlar fihristi.
- **NotebookLM Monster Assistant:** Canavar üretimi ve kural danışmanlığı için özel eğitilmiş yapay zeka asistanı bağlantısı.

## Reskin Reference Library

Dungeon Master'ların One Piece dünyasına özgü sıradan faunayı veya standart askeri birlikleri canlandırmak için sıfırdan yeni stat block yazmasına gerek yoktur. Resmi Monster Manual stat block'ları, yalnızca isimleri ve kozmetik görünümleri değiştirilerek (reskin) doğrudan masaya sürülebilir. Aşağıdaki liste, Grand Line ekosisteminde sıkça karşılaşılan canlıların doğrudan Monster Manual karşılıklarını içerir:

### Island and Sea Fauna Reskins

- **Kung Fu Dugong (Alabasta Coastal Seals):** Ape veya Bugbear (Unarmed Strike darbesi ve Martial Arts).
- **Bananawani (Alabasta Banana Croc):** Giant Crocodile
- **Adult Bananawani:** Giant Crocodile (Hit Points x2, Gargantuan boyut).
- **Yagara Bull (Water 7):** Plesiosaurus veya Giant Seahorse (Swim Speed 60 ft., binek olarak Large boyut).
- **King Bull (Water 7 Carrier Bull):** Triceratops veya Warhorse (Swim Speed 40 ft., Gargantuan boyut).
- **Lapahn (Drum Island Snow Rabbits):** Polar Bear veya Yeti (Standing Leap 20 ft., karda Burrow Speed 20 ft.).
- **South Bird:** Raven veya Blood Hawk (Compass Sense: Her zaman güney manyetik kutbunu işaret eder).
- **News Coo (World Government Mail Gull):** Blood Hawk veya Giant Eagle (Carrying Capacity x2, yorulmayan uçuş dayanıklılığı).
- **Sandora Dragon (Alabasta Desert Lizard):** Wyvern (Fly Speed yerine Burrow Speed 40 ft., zehirli iğne yerine ısırma).
- **Giant Desert Scorpion (Alabasta / Rusukaina):** Giant Scorpion
- **Island Cow (Mohmoo / Arlong Park):** Plesiosaurus veya Sea Serpent (Gargantuan boyut, Siege Monster).
- **Giant God Snake (Nola / Skypiea):** Giant Constrictor Snake veya Behir (Gargantuan boyut, Acid damage tükürüğü).
- **Sky Shark (Skypiea):** Hunter Shark (Beyaz Deniz bulutlarında Swim Speed 40 ft.).
- **Sky Fish (Skypiea):** Reef Shark (Beyaz Deniz bulutlarında Swim Speed 30 ft.).
- **Swarm of Sky Fish:** Swarm of Quippers (Beyaz Deniz bulutlarında Swim Speed 40 ft.).
- **Flying Fish (Flying Fish Riders):** Pteranodon (Swim Speed 40 ft. ve Fly Speed 60 ft.).
- **Fighting Fish (Dressrosa Colosseum):** Hunter Shark veya Killer Whale (Demir boynuz ile Ram saldırısı, Siege Monster).
- **Little Fighting Fish (Green Bit):** Reef Shark (Boynuz saldırısı ile Piercing damage).
- **Blue Gorilla (Blugori / Impel Down):** Ape veya Minotaur (Swim Speed 40 ft., Greataxe saldırısı).
- **Komainu (Wano Guardian Dog):** Dire Wolf veya Lion (Fire damage resistance, alevli pençe ve ısırık).
- **Komashika (Wano Deer):** Giant Elk
- **Komatori (Wano Giant Bird):** Giant Vulture
- **Cloud Fox (Suisui):** Weasel veya Cat
- **Snake Weapon (Kuja Warriors Snake Weapons):** Poisonous Snake veya Flying Snake (Katılaşma yeteneği: Longbow veya Spear silah formu).
- **Octopus Balloon:** Giant Octopus (Fly Speed 20 ft., sıcak havayla şişerek gemi taşıma).

### Military, Pirates and Underworld Reskins

- **Marine Recruits & Seamen:** Guard (Musket veya Pistol menzilli silahı).
- **Marine Petty Officers & Lieutenants:** Veteran veya Bandit Captain
- **Marine Captains:** Knight veya Gladiator
- **Marine Vice Admirals:** Champion veya Warlord (Rokushiki: Soru Bonus Action Dash/Disengage, Busoshoku Hardening: Force damage).
- **Pirate Recruits & Bandits:** Bandit
- **Pirate Captains:** Bandit Captain veya Berserker
- **Fish-Man Pirates & Thugs:** Bandit veya Sahuagin (Amphibious, Swim Speed 40 ft.).
- **Fish-Man Karate Apprentices:** Martial Arts Adept (Darbeleri Bludgeoning veya Cold damage verir).
- **Cipher Pol Lower Agents:** Spy veya Assassin (Rokushiki: Soru Bonus Action Dash, Tekkai Reaction AC bonusu).
- **Bounty Hunters:** Scout veya Veteran
- **Florian Triangle Entities & Ghost Ships:** Shadow, Specter veya Ghost

## Modular Monster Feature Library

Bu kütüphane, farklı sourcebook'lardaki 554 canavarın incelenmesiyle oluşturulmuştur. Sıradan ve hiçbir özel kural barındırmayan tekdüze saldırılar elenmiş; geriye kalan tüm zengin pasifler, alan etkili saldırılar, reaksiyonlar, bonus eylemler ve efsanevi eylemler 5 modüler tabloda toplanmıştır.

DM'ler karşılaşma hazırlarken Archetypal Monster Templates başlığı altındaki şablonları özelleştirirken veya yeni yaratıklar tasarlarken bu tablolardaki yeteneklerden ilham alabilirler.

> **DM Kalibrasyon Rehberi:**  
> Tablolardaki özellikler Monster Manual stat block'larından derlendiği için statik değerler (DC 10–27 vb.) içerir. Bu özellikleri **Archetypal Monster Templates**'e veya özel yaratıklara aktarırken yaratığın CR kademesine göre dinamik olarak ölçekleyin:
> - **Save DC:** `8 + Proficiency Bonus + Ability Modifier` (`[Save DC]`) olarak hesaplanır.
> - **Attack Roll:** `Proficiency Bonus + Ability Modifier` (`[Attack Bonus]`) değerine eşitlenir.
> - **Damage & Kontrol:** CR tablosundaki `[Hit Damage]` veya `[Recharge Damage]` bütçesi aşılmamalı; ağır kitle kontrolü etkileri (Swallow, Paralyzed vb.) yaratığın CR seviyesine uyarlanmalıdır.

### Table 1: Monster Traits

| Özellik İsmi | Bulunduğu Canavarlar | Açıklama |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Abduct** | Bugbear Stalker, Bugbear Warrior, Grell | The bugbear needn’t spend extra movement to move a creature it is grappling. |
| **Aberrant Ground** | Gibbering Mouther | The ground in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the mouther is Difficult Terrain. |
| **Aberrant Surge** | Tarkanan Ruffian | When the ruﬃan casts Witch Bolt, roll 1d8. On an even number, the ruﬃan gains a number of Temporary Hit Points equal to the number rolled. On an odd number, one creature of the ruﬃan’s choice within 30 feet of it takes Force damage equal to the number rolled; if no other creatures are in range, it takes the damage. |
| **Acid Absorption** | Clay Golem | Whenever the golem is subjected to Acid damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Acid damage dealt. |
| **Adhesive (Object Form Only)** | Mimic | The mimic adheres to anything that touches it. A Huge or smaller creature adhered to the mimic has the Grappled condition (escape DC 13). Ability checks made to escape this grapple have Disadvantage. |
| **Advanced Telepathy** | Flumph | The flumph perceives the content of any telepathic communication within 60 feet of it. |
| **Agile** | Deer, Rat | The deer doesn’t provoke an Opportunity Attack when it moves out of an enemy’s reach. |
| **Air Form** | Air Elemental, Invisible Stalker | The elemental can enter a creature’s space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so. |
| **Amorphous** | Black Pudding, Gray Ooze, Ochre Jelly, Psychic Gray Ooze, Shadow | The ooze can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so. |
| **Amphibious** | Ancient Bronze Dragon, Ancient Gold Dragon, Chuul, Crab, Giant Toad, Kuo-toa Archpriest, Kuo-toa Whip, Merfolk Wavebender, Merrow, Young Bronze Dragon, ... | The crab can breathe air and water. |
| **Astral Implosion** | Blob of Annihilation | If the blob is reduced to 0 Hit Points, it implodes and ejects any creatures and objects engulfed by it into the Astral Sea. The blob itself vanishes, leaving behind a layer of slime on everything that was within 600 feet of it. In 1d20 years, the blob reconstitutes on a random world in the Material Plane. |
| **Aura of Authority** | Emerald Claw Commander, Hobgoblin Captain, Hobgoblin Warlord | While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the hobgoblin, the hobgoblin and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the hobgoblin doesn’t have the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Aura of Bravery** | Questing Knight | Creatures of the knight’s choice in a 30-foot Emanation originating from it have Immunity to the Charmed and Frightened conditions while there. |
| **Aversion to Fire** | Flesh Golem | If the golem takes Fire damage, it has Disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks until the end of its next turn. |
| **Avoidance** | Displacer Beast | If the displacer beast is subjected to an effect that allows it to make a saving throw to take only half damage, it instead takes no damage if it succeeds on the save and half damage if it fails. It can’t use this trait if it has the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Barbed Hide** | Barbed Devil | At the start of each of its turns, the devil deals 5 (1d10) Piercing damage to any creature it is grappling or any creature grappling it. |
| **Beast of Burden** | Mule | The mule counts as one size larger for the purpose of determining its carrying capacity. |
| **Berserk** | Clay Golem, Flesh Golem | Whenever the golem starts its turn Bloodied, roll 1d6. On a 6, the golem goes berserk. On each of its turns while berserk, the golem attacks the nearest creature it can see. If no creature is near enough to move to and attack, the golem attacks an object. Once the golem goes berserk, it remains so until it is destroyed or it is no longer Bloodied. |
| **Blight Seeds** | Gulthias Blight | When it finishes a Long Rest, the blight expels 1d6 seeds into unoccupied spaces on the ground within 30 feet of itself. After 24 hours, the seeds become creatures under the blight’s control. Roll 1d8 for each seed to determine the creature it becomes: on 1–4, Twig Blight; on 5–6, Needle Blight; on 7–8, Vine Blight. |
| **Blood Frenzy** | Daask Bruiser, Sahuagin Baron, Sahuagin Priest, Sahuagin Warrior | The sahuagin has Advantage on attack rolls against any creature that doesn’t have all its Hit Points. |
| **Bloodied Frenzy** | Berserker, Berserker Commander | While Bloodied, the berserker has Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws. |
| **Bloodied Fury** | Boar, Giant Boar, Quaggoth, Quaggoth Thonot | While Bloodied, the boar has Advantage on attack rolls. |
| **Bloodied Wild Magic** | Noggle Wild Mage | While Bloodied, the noggle has a Fly Speed of 30 feet and it can choose for any damage it deals to be Force damage. |
| **Blurred Form** | Steam Mephit | Attack rolls against the mephit are made with Disadvantage unless the mephit has the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Bound** | Shield Guardian | The guardian is magically bound to an amulet. While the guardian and its amulet are on the same plane of existence, the amulet’s wearer can telepathically call the guardian to travel to it, and the guardian knows the distance and direction to the amulet. If the guardian is within 60 feet of the amulet’s wearer, half of any damage the wearer takes (round up) is transferred to the guardian. |
| **Captivated (Forager Only)** | Animal Lord, Animal Lord (Forager) | (Sub-effect of Lordly Presence) The target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn. While Charmed, the target has the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Celestial Restoration** | Guardian Naga | If the naga dies, it returns to life in 1d6 days and regains all its Hit Points unless Dispel Evil and Good is cast on its remains. |
| **Compression** | Octopus | The octopus can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so. |
| **Confer Fire Resistance** | Nightmare | The nightmare can grant Resistance to Fire damage to a rider while it is on the nightmare. |
| **Contortionist** | Manes Vaporspawn | The manes can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so. |
| **Corrosive Form** | Black Pudding, Gray Ooze | A creature that hits the pudding with a melee attack roll while within 5 feet of it takes 4 (1d8) Acid damage. Nonmagical ammunition is destroyed immediately after hitting the ooze and dealing damage. Any nonmagical weapon made of metal (or wood for Black Pudding) takes a cumulative −1 penalty to damage rolls immediately after dealing damage to the ooze. The weapon is destroyed if the penalty reaches −5 (removed by Mending). Black Pudding can eat through 2-foot-thick, nonmagical wood or metal in 1 minute. |
| **Coven Magic** | Arch-hag, Green Hag, Night Hag, Sea Hag | While within 30 feet of at least two hag allies, the hag can cast one of the following spells, requiring no Material components, using the spell’s normal casting time, and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 11): Augury, Find Familiar, Identify, Locate Object, Scrying, or Unseen Servant. The hag must finish a Long Rest before using this trait to cast that spell again. |
| **Death Burst** | Mud Mephit, Smoke Mephit | The mephit explodes when it dies (5-foot Emanation, DC 11 saving throw). Mud Mephit (Dexterity): Failure: The target has the Restrained condition until the end of its next turn. Smoke Mephit (Constitution): Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. |
| **Death Burst (1)** | Dust Mephit, Flaming Skeleton, Ice Mephit, Magma Mephit, Magmin, Steam Mephit | The mephit explodes when it dies. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 10, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the mephit. Failure: 5 (2d4) Fire damage. Success: Half damage. |
| **Death Burst (2)** | Gas Spore Fungus | The gas spore bursts when it dies. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 10, each creature in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the gas spore. Failure: The target takes 10 (3d6) Poison damage and has the Poisoned condition for 1d12 hours. Unless the Poisoned condition is removed, the target dies at the end of that time and sprouts 2d4 Tiny Gas Spore Fungi (each with 1 Hit Point). After 2d6 days, they become Large and have 13 Hit Points. |
| **Death Throes** | Balor | The balor explodes when it dies. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 20, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the balor. Failure: 31 (9d6) Fire damage plus 31 (9d6) Force damage. Success: Half damage. Failure or Success: If the balor dies outside the Abyss, it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Abyss. |
| **Demonic Restoration** | Barlgura, Chasme, Glabrezu, Goristro, Hezrou, Marilith, Nalfeshnee, Shadow Demon, Vrock, Yochlol | If the demon dies outside the Abyss, its body dissolves into ichor, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Abyss. |
| **Detect Intelligence** | Intellect Devourer | The intellect devourer magically senses the location of any creature within 300 feet of itself that has an Intelligence score of 3 or higher, regardless of interposing barriers. |
| **Detect Life** | Banshee | The banshee magically senses the direction of creatures up to 1 mile away that aren’t Constructs or Undead. |
| **Devil Fruit** | Devil Fruit Users, Officers, Warlords, Admirals, Emperors | The creature has consumed a specific Devil Fruit (Paramecia, Zoan, or Logia) and possesses all innate traits, form transformations, charge spells, and universal weaknesses (Powerless condition while submerged at least halfway in standing water or touching Seastone) detailed in that specific Devil Fruit's sheet. |
| **Diabolic Ward** | Harvester Devil | Attack rolls against the devil have Disadvantage. If the devil makes an attack roll, this trait is suppressed until the start of its next turn. |
| **Diabolical Restoration** | Barbed Devil, Bone Devil, Chain Devil, Erinyes, Harvester Devil, Horned Devil, Ice Devil, Pit Fiend, Speaker Devil | If the devil dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells. |
| **Disintegration** | Modron Duodrone, Modron Monodrone, Modron Pentadrone, Modron Quadrone, Modron Tridrone | If the modron dies, it disintegrates into dust, leaving behind anything it was wearing or carrying. |
| **Displacement** | Displacer Beast | Attack rolls against the displacer beast have Disadvantage, since it projects an illusion that makes it appear to be near its actual location. This trait is suppressed while the displacer beast has the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Divine Awareness** | Planetar, Solar | The planetar knows if it hears a lie. |
| **Earth Glide** | Dao, Earth Elemental, Elemental Cataclysm, Xorn | The elemental can burrow through nonmagical, unworked earth and stone. While doing so, the elemental doesn’t disturb the material it moves through. |
| **Eldritch Restoration** | Aboleth | If destroyed, the aboleth gains a new body in 5d10 days, reviving with all its Hit Points in the Far Realm or another location chosen by the DM. |
| **Elemental Restoration** | Dao, Djinni, Efreeti, Marid | If the dao dies outside the Elemental Plane of Earth, its body dissolves into dirt, and it gains a new body in 1d4 days, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere on the Plane of Earth. |
| **Ephemeral** | Will-o’-Wisp | The wisp can’t wear or carry anything. |
| **Ephemeral Movement** | Incarnation of Transience | The incarnation can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain, and its movement doesn’t provoke Opportunity Attacks. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object. |
| **Ethereal Sight** | Ghost, Phase Spider | The spider can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane while on the Material Plane and vice versa. |
| **Evasion** | Assassin, Bandit Crime Lord, Tarkanan Marauder | If the assassin is subjected to an effect that allows it to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, the assassin instead takes no damage if it succeeds on the save and only half damage if it fails. It can’t use this trait if it has the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Exalted Restoration** | Deva, Planetar, Solar | If the deva dies outside Mount Celestia, its body disappears, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in Mount Celestia. |
| **Fear Aura** | Pit Fiend | The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn’t have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend’s aura for 24 hours. |
| **Fear of Fire** | Abominable Yeti, Yeti | If the yeti takes Fire damage, it has Disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks until the end of its next turn. |
| **Fearful (Hunter Only)** | Animal Lord, Animal Lord (Hunter) | (Sub-effect of Lordly Presence) The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. |
| **Fetid Aura** | Swarm of Dretches | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 12, any creature that starts its turn in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the swarm. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the start of its next turn. While Poisoned, the target can take either an action or a Bonus Action on its turn, not both, and it can’t take Reactions. |
| **Fey Ancestry** | Umbragen Shadow-Walker, Vulkoori Stingblade, Vulkoori Venom Priest | Magic can’t put the stingblade to sleep. |
| **Fiendish Restoration** | Arcanaloth, Mezzoloth, Nycaloth, Rakshasa, Ultroloth | If the mezzoloth dies outside Gehenna, its body dissolves into ichor, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in Gehenna. |
| **Fiendish Restoration (1)** | Spirit Naga | If it dies, the naga returns to life in 1d6 days and regains all its Hit Points. Only a Wish spell can prevent this trait from functioning. |
| **Fire Absorption** | Iron Golem | Whenever the golem is subjected to Fire damage, it regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Fire damage dealt. |
| **Fire Aura** | Azer Pyromancer, Azer Sentinel, Balor, Fire Elemental, Salamander, Salamander Fire Snake, Salamander Inferno Master | At the end of each of the creature's turns, each creature in its Emanation takes Fire damage: Salamander / Azer: 3 (1d6) Fire in 5 ft.; Fire Elemental: 5 (1d10) Fire in 10 ft., and flammable objects and creatures in the Emanation start burning; Balor: 13 (3d8) Fire in 5 ft. |
| **Fire Form** | Fire Elemental | The elemental can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so, and it can enter a creature’s space and stop there. The first time it enters a creature’s space on a turn, that creature takes 5 (1d10) Fire damage. |
| **Flame Aura** | Brazen Gorgon | At the end of each of the gorgon’s turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the gorgon takes 13 (3d8) Fire damage. |
| **Flyby** | Animated Broom, Cockatrice Regent, Flying Snake, Gargoyle, Giant Wasp, Hippogriff, Owl, Peryton, Pteranodon, Spined Devil, ... | The owl doesn’t provoke Opportunity Attacks when it flies out of an enemy’s reach. |
| **Forbiddance** | Vampire Spawn, Vampire Warden | The vampire can’t enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant. |
| **Freeze** | Water Elemental | If the elemental takes Cold damage, its Speed decreases by 20 feet until the end of its next turn. |
| **Gibbering** | Gibbering Mouther | The mouther babbles incoherently while it doesn’t have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 10, any creature that starts its turn within 20 feet of the mouther while it is babbling. Failure: The target rolls 1d8 to determine what it does during the current turn: on a 1–4, the target does nothing; on a 5–6, the target takes no action or Bonus Action and uses all its movement to move in a random direction; on a 7–8, the target makes a melee attack against a randomly determined creature within its reach or does nothing if it can’t make such an attack. |
| **Greater Magic Resistance** | Rakshasa | The rakshasa automatically succeeds on saving throws against spells and other magical effects, and the attack rolls of spells automatically miss it. Without the rakshasa’s permission, no spell can observe the rakshasa remotely or detect its thoughts, creature type, or alignment. |
| **Haki** | Marine Officers, Vice Admirals, New World Pirates, Warlords, Emperors | The creature possesses spiritual awareness and passive mastery in one or more Haki Colors: Color of Armament (attacks bypass physical damage resistances with Force damage), Color of Observation (enhanced senses, Blindsight, or Advantage on Initiative and Wisdom checks), or Color of the Supreme King (Immunity to the Charmed and Frightened conditions), as detailed in [Chapter 8: Haki](#chapter-8-haki). |
| **Haunted Zone** | Haunting Revenant | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, any creature that casts a spell while inside the revenant’s space. Failure: The spell fails and is wasted. |
| **Heat Aura** | Remorhaz, Young Remorhaz | At the end of each of the remorhaz’s turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the remorhaz takes 11 (2d10) Fire damage. |
| **Hellish Restoration** | Lemure, Swarm of Lemures | If the lemure dies in the Nine Hells, it revives with all its Hit Points in 1d10 days unless it is killed by a creature under the effects of a Bless spell or its remains are sprinkled with Holy Water. |
| **Hold Breath** | Crocodile, Giant Crocodile, Hippopotamus, Hydra, Killer Whale, Plesiosaurus | The crocodile can hold its breath for 1 hour. |
| **Hustle** | Boromar Smuggler, Boromar Underboss, Ghallanda Troubleshooter | The smuggler can move through the space of any creature that is of a larger size, but it can’t stop there. |
| **Ice Walk** | Adult White Dragon, Ancient White Dragon, White Dragon Wyrmling, Young White Dragon | The dragon can move across and climb icy surfaces without needing to make an ability check. Additionally, Difficult Terrain composed of ice or snow doesn’t cost it extra movement. |
| **Illumination** | Azer Pyromancer, Azer Sentinel, Brazen Gorgon, Fire Elemental, Flameskull, Flaming Skeleton, Giant Fire Beetle, Nightmare, Will-o’-Wisp | The beetle sheds Bright Light in a 10-foot radius and Dim Light for an additional 10 feet. |
| **Immobile Restoration** | Vampire Warden | If the vampire drops to 0 Hit Points while it doesn’t have the Petrified condition, it turns to stone, regains 50 Hit Points, and has the Petrified condition for 1 hour. |
| **Incorporeal Movement** | Banshee, Empyrean Iota (Celestial), Empyrean Iota (Fiend), Ghost, Poltergeist, Shadow Demon, Specter, Will-o’-Wisp, Wraith | The empyrean can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object. |
| **Inscrutable** | Sphinx of Lore, Sphinx of Secrets, Sphinx of Valor | No magic can observe the sphinx remotely or detect its thoughts without its permission. Wisdom (Insight) checks made to ascertain its intentions or sincerity are made with Disadvantage. |
| **Invisibility** | Invisible Stalker | The stalker has the Invisible condition. |
| **Invisible in Water** | Water Weird | The water weird has the Invisible condition while fully immersed in water. |
| **Iron Scent** | Rust Monster | The rust monster can pinpoint the location of ferrous metal within 30 feet of itself. |
| **Jumper** | Cat | The cat's jump distance is determined using its Dexterity rather than its Strength. |
| **Legendary Resistance (3/Day)** | Balor, Death Knight, Unicorn | If the unicorn fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead. |
| **Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair)** | Adult Blue Dragon, Adult Gold Dragon, Adult Red Dragon, Adult White Dragon, Beholder, Death Tyrant, Shadow Dragon (Huge), Shadow Dragon (Large), Sphinx of Valor, Vampire, ... | If the aboleth fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead. |
| **Legendary Resistance (4/Day)** | Animal Lord, Animal Lord (Forager), Animal Lord (Hunter), Animal Lord (Sage), Blob of Annihilation, Colossus, Elemental Cataclysm, Empyrean (Celestial), Empyrean (Fiend), Pit Fiend, ... | If the animal lord fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead. |
| **Legendary Resistance (4/Day, or 5/Day in Lair)** | Ancient Black Dragon, Ancient Blue Dragon, Ancient Bronze Dragon, Ancient Copper Dragon, Ancient Gold Dragon, Ancient Green Dragon, Ancient Red Dragon, Ancient Silver Dragon, Arch-hag, Kraken, ... | If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead. |
| **Legendary Resistance (6/Day)** | Tarrasque | If the tarrasque fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead. |
| **Life Suppression** | Dracolich, Dracolich (Gargantuan) | Creatures within 60 feet of the dracolich can’t regain Hit Points. |
| **Light Sensitivity** | Cloaker, Shadow Demon | While in Bright Light, the demon has Disadvantage on ability checks and attack rolls. |
| **Lightning Absorption** | Flesh Golem, Shambling Mound | Whenever the golem is subjected to Lightning damage, it regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Lightning damage dealt. |
| **Limited Amphibiousness** | Sahuagin Baron, Sahuagin Priest, Sahuagin Warrior | The sahuagin can breathe air and water, but it must be submerged at least once every 4 hours to avoid suffocating outside water. |
| **Living Shadow** | Juvenile Shadow Dragon, Shadow Dragon (Huge), Shadow Dragon (Large) | While in Dim Light or Darkness, the dragon has Resistance to damage that isn’t Force, Psychic, or Radiant. |
| **Loathsome Limbs (4/Day)** | Troll | If the troll ends any turn Bloodied and took 15+ Slashing damage during that turn, one of the troll’s limbs is severed, falls into the troll’s space, and becomes a Troll Limb. The limb acts immediately after the troll’s turn. The troll has 1 Exhaustion level for each missing limb, and it grows replacement limbs the next time it regains Hit Points. |
| **Lordly Presence** | Animal Lord, Animal Lord (Forager), Animal Lord (Hunter), Animal Lord (Sage) | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 20, any enemy that starts its turn in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the animal lord. Failure: The target suffers one of the following effects (chosen based on the creature's archetype or DM's choice): Captivated (the target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn; while Charmed, it has the Incapacitated condition); Fearful (the target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn); or Mired (the target takes 10 (3d6) Psychic damage and subtracts 1d4 from its saving throws until the end of its next turn). |
| **Lucky** | Jorasco Medic | When the medic rolls a 1 on the d20 of a D20 Test, it can reroll the die, and it must use the new roll. |
| **Magic Resistance** | Animal Lord (Sage), Faerie Dragon Adult, Iron Golem, Mezzoloth, Pixie, Pseudodragon, Quasit, Red Slaad, Solar, Speaker Devil, ... | The slaad has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects. |
| **Magic Rope** | Erinyes | The erinyes has a magic rope. While bearing it, the erinyes can use the Entangling Rope action. The rope has AC 20, HP 90, and Immunity to Poison and Psychic damage. The rope turns to dust if reduced to 0 Hit Points, if it is 5+ feet away from the erinyes for 1 hour or more, or if the erinyes dies. If the rope is damaged or destroyed, the erinyes can fully restore it when finishing a Short or Long Rest. |
| **Marshal Undead** | Death Knight, Death Knight Aspirant | Undead creatures of the aspirant’s choice (excluding itself) in a 60-foot Emanation originating from it have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws. It can’t use this trait if it has the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Mechanical Determination** | Domestic Wonder | If damage reduces the wonder to 0 Hit Points, it makes a Constitution saving throw with a DC of 5 plus the damage taken unless the damage is Lightning or from a Critical Hit. On a successful save, the wonder drops to 1 Hit Point instead. |
| **Mimicry** | Green Hag, Kenku, Merrow Doublespeaker, Raven | The raven can mimic simple sounds it has heard, such as a whisper or chitter. A hearer can discern the sounds are imitations with a successful DC 10 Wisdom (Insight) check. |
| **Mired** | Animal Lord (Sage) | (Sub-effect of Lordly Presence) The target takes 10 (3d6) Psychic damage, and the target is magically bewildered until the end of its next turn. While bewildered, the target subtracts 1d4 from its saving throws. |
| **Mired (Sage Only)** | Animal Lord | (Sub-effect of Lordly Presence) The target takes 10 (3d6) Psychic damage, and the target is magically bewildered until the end of its next turn. While bewildered, the target subtracts 1d4 from its saving throws. |
| **Misty Escape** | Vampire, Vampire Infernalist | If the vampire drops to 0 Hit Points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can’t use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed. While it has 0 Hit Points in mist form, it can’t return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and has the Paralyzed condition until it regains any Hit Points, and it regains 1 Hit Point after spending 1 hour there. |
| **Mucus Cloud** | Aboleth | While underwater, the aboleth is surrounded by mucus. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the aboleth at the end of the aboleth’s turn. Failure: The target is cursed. Until the curse ends, the target’s skin becomes slimy, the target can breathe air and water, and it can’t regain Hit Points unless it is underwater. While the cursed creature is outside a body of water, the creature takes 6 (1d12) Acid damage at the end of every 10 minutes unless moisture is applied to its skin before those minutes have passed. |
| **Multiple Heads** | Hydra | The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead. At the end of each of its turns when it has at least one living head, the hydra grows two heads for each of its heads that died since its last turn, unless it has taken Fire damage since its last turn. The hydra regains 20 Hit Points when it grows new heads. |
| **Ooze Cube** | Gelatinous Cube | The cube fills its entire space and is transparent. Other creatures can enter that space, but a creature that does so is subjected to the cube’s Engulf and has Disadvantage on the saving throw. Creatures inside the cube have Total Cover, and the cube can hold one Large creature or up to four Medium or Small creatures inside itself at a time. As an action, a creature within 5 feet of the cube can pull a creature or an object out of the cube by succeeding on a DC 12 Strength (Athletics) check, and the puller takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage. |
| **Pack Tactics** | Blood Hawk, Dire Wolf, Giant Rat, Hobgoblin Warrior, Tough, Twig Blight, Vadalis Heir, Warrior Infantry, Winter Wolf, Wolf, ... | The baboon has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the baboon’s allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn’t have the Incapacitated condition. |
| **Probing Telepathy** | Aboleth | If a creature the aboleth can see communicates telepathically with the aboleth, the aboleth learns the creature’s greatest desires. |
| **Prone Deficiency** | Flumph | If the flumph receives the Prone condition, roll a die. On an odd number, it has the Incapacitated condition. At the end of each of its turns, the flumph makes a DC 10 Dexterity saving throw, ending the Incapacitated condition on a success. |
| **Reactive** | Marilith | The marilith can take one Reaction on every turn of combat. |
| **Reactive Heads** | Hydra | For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra Reaction that can be used only for Opportunity Attacks. |
| **Reflective Carapace** | Tarrasque | If the tarrasque is targeted by a Magic Missile spell or a spell that requires a ranged attack roll, roll 1d6. On a 1–5, the tarrasque is unaffected. On a 6, the tarrasque is unaffected and reflects the spell, turning the caster into the target. |
| **Regeneration** | Revenant, Troll, Troll Limb | The limb regains 5 Hit Points at the start of each of its turns. If the limb takes Acid or Fire damage, this trait doesn’t function on the limb’s next turn. The limb dies only if it starts its turn with 0 Hit Points and doesn’t regenerate. |
| **Regeneration (1)** | Blue Slaad, Death Slaad, Gray Slaad, Green Slaad, Oni, Red Slaad, Shield Guardian | The slaad regains 10 Hit Points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 Hit Point. |
| **Running Leap** | Lion, Saber-Toothed Tiger | With a 10-foot running start, the lion can Long Jump up to 25 feet. |
| **Running Water** | Vampire, Vampire Infernalist, Vampire Spawn, Vampire Umbral Lord, Vampire Warden | The vampire takes 20 Acid damage if it ends its turn in running water. |
| **Sense Magic** | Chuul | The chuul senses magic within 120 feet of itself. This trait otherwise works like the Detect Magic spell but isn’t itself magical. |
| **Shadow Escape** | Vampire Umbral Lord | If the vampire drops to 0 Hit Points outside its resting place, it teleports into its resting place unless it is in running water or sunlight. If it can’t teleport, it is destroyed. Once inside its resting place, it has the Paralyzed condition for 1 hour, after which it regains 1 Hit Point. |
| **Shark Telepathy** | Sahuagin Baron, Sahuagin Priest, Sahuagin Warrior | The sahuagin can magically control sharks within 120 feet of itself, using a special telepathy. |
| **Shielded Mind** | Couatl | The couatl’s thoughts can’t be read by any means, and other creatures can communicate with it telepathically only if it allows them. |
| **Sickening Vapors** | Manes Vaporspawn | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 12, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the manes at the end of the manes’s turn. Failure: The target has the Incapacitated condition until the end of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this manes’s Sickening Vapors for 24 hours. |
| **Siege Monster** | Colossus, Earth Elemental, Elemental Cataclysm, Goristro, Kraken, Noggle Ransacker, Tarrasque, Treant | The noggle deals double damage to objects and structures. |
| **Soul Bag** | Night Hag | The hag has a soul bag. While holding or carrying the bag, the hag can use its Nightmare Haunting action. |
| **Soul Gem** | Dracolich, Dracolich (Gargantuan) | The dracolich has a magical gem. If the dracolich is destroyed while the gem is on the same plane of existence as it, the dracolich gains a new body in 1d20 days, regaining all its Hit Points and appearing within 5 feet of the gem. |
| **Soul Tome** | Arcanaloth | The arcanaloth has a magic tome. While holding or carrying the tome, the arcanaloth can use its Banishing Claw action. |
| **Speak with Beasts and Plants** | Dryad | The dryad can communicate with Beasts and Plants as if they shared a language. |
| **Speak with Frogs and Toads** | Bullywug Bog Sage, Bullywug Warrior | The bullywug can communicate simple concepts to frogs and toads when it speaks in Bullywug. |
| **Spell Immunity** | Helmed Horror | The helmed horror is immune to three spells chosen by its creator. Typical choices include Heat Metal, Lightning Bolt, and Magic Missile. |
| **Spell Storing** | Shield Guardian | A spellcaster who wears the guardian’s amulet can cause the guardian to store one spell of level 4 or lower. To do so, the wearer must cast the spell on the guardian while within 5 feet of it. The spell has no effect but is stored within the guardian. Any previously stored spell is lost when a new spell is stored. The guardian can cast the spell stored with any parameters set by the original caster, requiring no spell components and using the caster’s spellcasting ability. The stored spell is then lost. |
| **Spider Climb** | Ettercap, Giant Lizard, Giant Spider, Giant Wolf Spider, Lizard, Ochre Jelly, Piercer, Roper, Spider, Vampire Spawn, ... | The lizard can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check. |
| **Spirit Jar** | Lich | If destroyed, the lich reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its Hit Points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the lich’s lair. |
| **Spiteful Escape** | Arch-hag | When the hag drops to 0 Hit Points, it dies only if it is within 30 feet of its anathema (a thing the DM chooses as the hag’s most hated thing). Otherwise, the hag drops to 1 Hit Point and teleports to a harmless demiplane, and it can’t return to the plane it left for 2d6 days. When the hag teleports away, each creature within 60 feet of the space it left is cursed. Until the curse ends, a creature has Disadvantage on ability checks and saving throws, and the hag knows its location anywhere in the multiverse. |
| **Stake to the Heart** | Vampire, Vampire Infernalist, Vampire Spawn, Vampire Umbral Lord, Vampire Warden | If a weapon that deals Piercing damage is driven into the vampire’s heart while it has the Incapacitated condition in its resting place, the vampire has the Paralyzed condition until the weapon is removed (Vampire Spawn is destroyed instead). |
| **Standing Leap** | Frog, Giant Frog, Giant Toad | The frog’s Long Jump is up to 10 feet and its High Jump is up to 5 feet with or without a running start. |
| **Stench** | Ghast, Ghast Gravecaller, Hezrou, Troglodyte | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 10 (Ghast, 5-ft. Emanation), DC 12 (Troglodyte, 5-ft. Emanation), or DC 16 (Hezrou, 10-ft. Emanation), any creature that starts its turn in the Emanation. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the start of its next turn. Success: Immune for 1 hour (troglodytes) or 24 hours (ghasts). |
| **Succubus Form** | Incubus | When the incubus finishes a Long Rest, it can shape-shift into a Succubus, using that stat block instead of this one. Any equipment it’s wearing or carrying isn’t transformed. |
| **Sun Sickness** | Myconid Adult, Myconid Sovereign, Myconid Sprout | While in sunlight, the myconid has Disadvantage on D20 Tests. The myconid dies if it spends more than 1 hour in sunlight. |
| **Sunlight** | Vampire, Vampire Infernalist, Vampire Spawn, Vampire Umbral Lord, Vampire Warden | The vampire takes 20 Radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has Disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks. |
| **Sunlight Hypersensitivity** | Vampire Nightbringer | The vampire takes 10 Radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has Disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks. |
| **Sunlight Sensitivity** | Juvenile Shadow Dragon, Kobold Warrior, Kuo-toa Archpriest, Kuo-toa Whip, Shadow Dragon (Huge), Shadow Dragon (Large), Troglodyte, Umbragen Shadow-Walker, Wight, Winged Kobold, ... | While in sunlight, the kobold has Disadvantage on ability checks and attack rolls. |
| **Sunlight Weakness** | Shadow | While in sunlight, the shadow has Disadvantage on D20 Tests. |
| **Swarm** | Swarm of Bats, Swarm of Crawling Claws, Swarm of Dretches, Swarm of Insects, Swarm of Larvae, Swarm of Lemures, Swarm of Piranhas, Swarm of Ravens, Swarm of Stirges, Swarm of Venomous Snakes, ... | The swarm can occupy another creature’s space and vice versa, and the swarm can move through any opening large enough for a Tiny bat. The swarm can’t regain Hit Points or gain Temporary Hit Points. |
| **Telepathic Bond** | Homunculus | While the homunculus is on the same plane of existence as its master, the two of them can communicate telepathically with each other. |
| **Telepathic Shroud** | Flumph | The flumph’s thoughts can’t be read by any means, and magic can’t detect its location or observe it remotely. |
| **Training** | Commoner | The commoner has proficiency in one skill of the DM’s choice and has Advantage whenever it makes an ability check using that skill. |
| **Transparent** | Gelatinous Cube | Even when the cube is in plain sight, a creature must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom (Perception) check to notice the cube if the creature hasn’t witnessed the cube move or otherwise act. |
| **Treasure Sense** | Xorn | The xorn can pinpoint the location of precious metals and stones within 60 feet of itself. |
| **Troll Spawn** | Troll Limb | The limb uncannily has the same senses as a whole troll. If the limb isn’t destroyed within 24 hours, roll 1d12. On a 12, the limb turns into a Troll. Otherwise, the limb withers away. |
| **Tunneler** | Ankheg, Purple Worm, Umber Hulk | The ankheg can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake. |
| **Undead Fortitude** | Beholder Zombie, Ogre Zombie, Zombie | If damage reduces the zombie to 0 Hit Points, it makes a Constitution saving throw (DC 5 plus the damage taken) unless the damage is Radiant or from a Critical Hit. On a successful save, the zombie drops to 1 Hit Point instead. |
| **Undead Restoration** | Demilich, Flameskull | If the flameskull is destroyed, it regains all its Hit Points in 1 hour unless Holy Water is sprinkled on its remains or the Dispel Evil and Good spell is cast on them. |
| **Undead Restoration (1)** | Graveyard Revenant, Haunting Revenant, Revenant | If the revenant dies, it revives 24 hours later in a different body unless Dispel Evil and Good is cast on its corpse. If it revives, it animates a Humanoid corpse elsewhere on the same plane of existence; it now looks different but uses the same stat block and returns with all its Hit Points. |
| **Undead Restoration (2)** | Mummy Lord | If destroyed, the mummy gains a new body in 24 hours if its heart is intact, reviving with all its Hit Points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the mummy’s lair. The heart is a Tiny object that has AC 17, HP 10, and Immunity to all damage except Fire. |
| **Undead Restoration (3)** | Death Knight | If the death knight is destroyed before it atones for its evil, it gains a new body in 1d10 days, reviving with all its Hit Points. The new body appears in a location significant to the death knight. |
| **Vampiric Connection** | Vampire Familiar | While the familiar and its vampire master are on the same plane of existence, the vampire can communicate with the familiar telepathically, and the vampire can perceive through the familiar’s senses. |
| **Vile Appearance** | Sea Hag | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 11, any Beast or Humanoid that starts its turn within 30 feet of the hag and can see the hag’s true form. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this hag’s Vile Appearance for 24 hours. |
| **Water Breathing** | Giant Octopus, Giant Seahorse, Giant Shark, Giant Squid, Hunter Shark, Octopus, Piranha, Reef Shark, Seahorse, Swarm of Piranhas | The octopus can breathe only underwater. |
| **Water Form** | Water Elemental | The elemental can enter an enemy’s space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so. |
| **Water Susceptibility** | Fire Elemental | The elemental takes 3 (1d6) Cold damage for every 5 feet the elemental moves in water or for every gallon of water splashed on it. |
| **Web Walker** | Drider, Ettercap, Giant Spider, Phase Spider, Spider, Yochlol | The spider ignores movement restrictions caused by webs, and the spider knows the location of any other creature in contact with the same web. |
| **Wind-Up Operation** | Domestic Wonder | The wonder has the Unconscious condition until another creature winds it with the wonder’s unique key for 1 minute. Once wound, the wonder operates for 10 days or until a creature touches the wonder with its key as a Utilize action to deactivate it, after which the wonder has the Unconscious condition until it is wound again. |
| **Wishes** | Dao, Djinni, Efreeti, Marid | The dao has a 30 percent chance of knowing the Wish spell. If the dao knows it, the dao can cast it only on behalf of a non-genie creature who communicates a wish in a way the dao can understand. If the dao casts the spell for the creature, the dao suffers none of the spell’s stress. Once the dao has cast it three times, the dao can’t do so again for 365 days. |
| **Words of Reason** | Sivis Scribe | Allies in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the scribe have Advantage on saving throws to avoid or end the Charmed or Frightened condition. This trait doesn’t function if the scribe has the Incapacitated condition. |

Failure: The target is possessed by the ghost; the ghost disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The ghost now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The ghost can’t be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The ghost’s game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target’s Speed, as well as the target’s Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers. The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points, the ghost ends it as a Bonus Action, or the ghost is turned or forced out by an effect like Dispel Evil and Good. When the possession ends, the ghost reappears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the body, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. |

| **Surge** | Water Weird | Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 10 ft. Hit: 13 (3d6 + 3) Cold damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 13), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends. |
| **Swallow** | Giant Frog | The frog swallows a Small or smaller target it is grappling. While swallowed, the target isn’t Grappled but has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, and it has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the frog. In addition, the target takes 5 (2d4) Acid damage at the end of each of the frog's turns. While swallowing the target, the frog can’t use Bite. If the frog dies, the swallowed target is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 5 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition. |
| **Swallow (1)** | Giant Toad | The toad swallows a Medium or smaller target it is grappling. While swallowed, the target isn’t Grappled but has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, and it has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the toad. In addition, the target takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the end of each of the toad’s turns. The toad can have only one target swallowed at a time, and it can’t use Bite while it has a swallowed target. If the toad dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 5 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition. |
| **Swallow (2)** | Kraken | Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 25, one creature Grappled by the kraken (it can have up to four creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: 23 (3d8 + 10) Piercing damage. If the target is Large or smaller, it is swallowed and no longer Grappled. A swallowed creature has the Restrained condition, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the kraken, and takes 24 (7d6) Acid damage at the start of each of its turns. If the kraken takes 50 damage or more on a single turn from a swallowed creature, the kraken must succeed on a DC 25 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, which land Prone in unoccupied spaces within 10 feet of the kraken. If the kraken dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 15 feet of movement, exiting Prone. |
| **Swarm of Grasping Hands** | Swarm of Crawling Claws | Melee Attack Roll: +4, reach 5 ft. Hit: 20 (4d8 + 2) Necrotic damage, or 11 (2d8 + 2) Necrotic damage if the swarm is Bloodied. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition. |
| **Swarm of Proboscises** | Swarm of Stirges | Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. Hit: 14 (2d10 + 3) Piercing damage, or 8 (1d10 + 3) Piercing damage if the swarm is Bloodied. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature in the swarm’s space, the target has the Grappled condition (escape DC 13). Until the grapple ends, the target takes 7 (2d6) Necrotic damage at the end of each of its turns. |
| **Tail** | Ankylosaurus, Dragon Turtle, Giant Crocodile, Tarrasque, Tyrannosaurus Rex | Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 10 ft. Hit: 9 (1d10 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Huge or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition. |
| **Talons** | Aarakocra Skirmisher, Peryton | Melee Attack Roll: +4, reach 5 ft. Hit: 4 (1d4 + 2) Slashing damage, or 9 (3d4 + 2) Slashing damage if the aarakocra moved 30+ feet straight toward the target immediately before the hit. |

The death tyrant can also exert fine control on objects with this ray, such as manipulating a tool or opening a door or container. |

### Table 3: Monster Bonus Actions

| Özellik İsmi | Bulunduğu Canavarlar | Açıklama |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Absorb Body** | Violet Fungus Necrohulk | Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, one Medium or Small creature the necrohulk can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is pulled into the necrohulk’s space and becomes grafted to its body. The necrohulk can have only one target grafted at a time. |
| **Aim** | Scout Captain | The scout has Advantage on the next attack roll it makes during the current turn. |
| **Antimagic Cone** | Beholder | The beholder’s central eye emits an antimagic wave in a 150-foot Cone. Until the start of the beholder’s next turn, that area acts as an Antimagic Field spell, and that area works against the beholder’s own Eye Rays. |
| **Aquatic Charge** | Sahuagin Warrior | The sahuagin swims up to its Swim Speed straight toward an enemy it can see. |
| **Blazing Movement** | Salamander Inferno Master | The salamander moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, fire fills a 5-foot Emanation originating from the salamander. When the Emanation enters a creature’s space, that creature takes 7 (2d6) Fire damage. A creature can take this damage only once per turn. |
| **Brace Up** | Belligerent Brawler, Boxer, Streetfighter, Wrestler | The streetfighter gains 9 (2d8) Temporary Hit Points. |
| **Bubble Dash** | Giant Seahorse | While underwater, the seahorse moves up to half its Swim Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. |
| **Burst of Wonder (Recharge 5–6)** | Pixie Wonderbringer | The pixie casts Entangle, Polymorph, or Tasha’s Hideous Laughter, requiring no Material components and using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |
| **Captain’s Charm** | Pirate Captain | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature the pirate can see within 30 feet. Failure: The target has the Charmed condition until the start of the pirate’s next turn. |
| **Charge** | Goristro, Lizardfolk Sovereign, Troll, Xorn | The lizardfolk moves up to its Speed or Swim Speed straight toward an enemy it can see. |
| **Charm (Recharge 5–6)** | Vampire | The vampire casts Charm Person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The Charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire’s Bite, the damage of which doesn’t end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was Charmed by the vampire. |
| **Combo** | Belligerent Brawler, Boxer, Streetfighter, Wrestler | The streetfighter makes one Fisticuffs attack. |
| **Confusing Gaze (Recharge 5–6)** | Umber Hulk | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: The target can’t take Reactions until the start of the umber hulk’s next turn, and the target rolls 1d8 to determine what it does on its next turn: on a 1–4, the target does nothing; on a 5–6, the target takes no action or Bonus Action and uses all its movement to move in a random direction; on a 7–8, the target makes a melee attack against a random creature within its reach or does nothing if it can’t make such an attack. |
| **Consume Life** | Will-o’-Wisp | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 10, one living creature the wisp can see within 5 feet that has 0 Hit Points. Failure: The target dies, and the wisp regains 10 (3d6) Hit Points. |

| **Deathless Agility** | Vampire Familiar, Vampire Spawn | The familiar takes the Dash or Disengage action. |
| **Divine Aid (1/Day)** | Priest Acolyte | The priest casts Bless, Healing Word, or Sanctuary, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |
| **Divine Aid (2/Day)** | Couatl, Deva, Planetar | The couatl casts Bless, Lesser Restoration, or Sanctuary, requiring no spell components and using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |

| **Drone** | Chasme | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 12, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the chasme (demons automatically succeed on this save). Failure: The target has the Unconscious condition and repeats the save at the end of each of its turns. The target succeeds automatically after 10 minutes or if it takes damage or a creature within 5 feet of it takes an action to empty a flask of Holy Water on it. Success: The target is immune to this chasme’s Drone for 24 hours. |

Otherwise, the target takes 18 (4d8) Psychic damage. |

Second Failure: The target has the Petrified condition instead of the Restrained condition. |
| **Phantasms (Recharge after a Short or Long Rest)** | Cloaker | The cloaker casts the Mirror Image spell, requiring no spell components and using Wisdom as the spellcasting ability. The spell ends early if the cloaker starts or ends its turn in Bright Light. |
| **Prowl (Tiger or Hybrid Form Only)** | Weretiger | The weretiger moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. At the end of this movement, the weretiger can take the Hide action. |
| **Psi-Powered Leap (2/Day)** | Githzerai Monk, Githzerai Zerth | The githzerai casts Jump, requiring no spell components and using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |
| **Quick Grapple** | Bugbear Stalker | Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature the bugbear can see within 10 feet. Failure: The target has the Grappled condition (escape DC 13). |
| **Rally (1/Day)** | Pirate Admiral | The pirate chooses up to three other creatures it can see within 30 feet. Until the start of the pirate’s next turn, the targets have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws. |
| **Rampage (1/Day)** | Giant Hyena, Gnoll Warrior | Immediately after dealing damage to a creature that is already Bloodied, the gnoll moves up to half its Speed, and it makes one Rend attack. |
| **Rampage (2/Day)** | Gnoll Demoniac, Gnoll Fang of Yeenoghu, Gnoll Pack Lord | Immediately after dealing damage to a creature that is already Bloodied, the gnoll moves up to half its Speed, and it makes one Bone Whip attack. |
| **Reel** | Ettercap | The ettercap pulls one creature within 30 feet of itself that is Restrained by its Web Strand up to 25 feet straight toward itself. |
| **Sanguine Drain** | Vampire Umbral Lord | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 18, one creature the vampire can see within 30 feet that isn’t a Construct or an Undead. Failure: 14 (4d6) Necrotic damage. The target’s Hit Point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the damage taken, and the vampire regains Hit Points equal to that amount. |
| **Shadow Cloak** | Shadow-Marked Agent | The agent has the Invisible condition until the start of its next turn. This invisibility ends early immediately after the agent makes an attack roll. |
| **Shadow Stealth** | Juvenile Shadow Dragon, Manes Vaporspawn, Shadow, Shadow Demon, Shadow Dragon (Huge), Shadow Dragon (Large), Vampire Nightbringer | While in Dim Light or Darkness, the shadow takes the Hide action. |
| **Shadowy Teleport** | Nycaloth | The nycaloth has the Invisible condition for 1 minute, and it teleports up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. The condition ends early immediately after it deals damage. |
| **Shape-Shift** | Doppelganger, Jackalwere, Werebear, Wereboar, Wererat, Weretiger, Werewolf | The jackalwere shape-shifts into a Medium human or a Medium jackal-humanoid hybrid, or it returns to its true form (that of a Small jackal). Other than its size, its game statistics are the same in each form. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying isn’t transformed. |
| **Shape-Shift (Vampire)** | Vampire, Vampire Infernalist | The vampire shape-shifts into a Tiny bat or a Medium cloud of mist, or it returns to its true vampire form. Its game statistics are the same in each form, except where specified (bat form has Fly Speed 30 ft.; mist form has Fly Speed 20 ft. (hover), Resistance to physical damage, can pass through 1-inch spaces, and can't speak or manipulate objects). Equipment isn't transformed. |
| **Shape-Shift (1)** | Animal Lord, Animal Lord (Hunter), Animal Lord (Sage), Green Slaad, Mimic, Night Hag, Succubus, Yuan-ti Abomination, Yuan-ti Malison (Type 1), Yuan-ti Malison (Type 2), ... | The mimic shape-shifts to resemble a Medium or Small object while retaining its game statistics, or it returns to its true blob form. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying isn’t transformed. |
| **Shape-Shift (2)** | Incarnation of Transience | The incarnation changes its size to Medium, Large, or Huge. |
| **Shield of Faith (2/Day)** | Kuo-toa Archpriest, Kuo-toa Whip | The kuo-toa casts Shield of Faith, using Wisdom as the spellcasting ability. |
| **Slayer’s Puppet (1/Day)** | Dreaming Dark Mindkiller | The mindkiller casts Dominate Person, requiring no spell components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17). |
| **Slow (Recharge 5–6)** | Stone Golem | The golem casts the Slow spell, requiring no spell components and using Constitution as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17). |
| **Spirit Wail (Recharge 5–6)** | Death Cultist | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, each creature in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the cultist. Failure: 14 (4d6) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only. |
| **Spiritual Weapon (2/Day)** | Cultist Fanatic | The cultist casts the Spiritual Weapon spell, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |
| **Spur Beast** | Vadalis Heir | The heir targets a Beast it can see within 30 feet. The target can take a Reaction to move up to half its Speed and make one melee attack. |
| **Superior Invisibility** | Faerie Dragon Adult, Faerie Dragon Youth | The dragon casts Greater Invisibility on itself, requiring no spell components and using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |
| **Swallow (Bonus Action)** | Behir, Purple Worm, Remorhaz, Tarrasque | Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 17–20, one creature Grappled by the monster (Large or smaller; Huge for Tarrasque). Failure: Swallowed and no longer Grappled; has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, Total Cover, and takes 21 (6d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the monster's turns (plus 14 Fire for Remorhaz; 42 Acid for Tarrasque). If the monster takes sufficient damage from inside on a single turn (30+ for Behir/Worm/Remorhaz; 60+ for Tarrasque), it must succeed on a DC 14–21 Constitution save or regurgitate all swallowed creatures Prone. Swallowed creatures escape corpse on death. |
| **Swoop (Recharge 5–6)** | Roc | If the roc has a creature Grappled, the roc flies up to half its Fly Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks and drops that creature. |
| **Tactical Charge** | Warrior Commander | The warrior moves up to half its Speed straight toward an enemy it can see without provoking Opportunity Attacks. |
| **Tear Through Space** | Tarkanan Marauder | The marauder teleports to a space it can see within 30 feet, appearing in a dazzling flash. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the marauder when it appears. Failure: The target has the Blinded condition until the end of the marauder’s next turn. |
| **Teleport** | Incarnation of Vibrance | The incarnation teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. The incarnation can’t teleport while it has a creature Grappled. |
| **Teleport (Arcanaloth)** | Arcanaloth | The arcanaloth teleports up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. |
| **Teleport (Balor)** | Balor | The balor teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and it can teleport one willing demon within 10 feet of itself to an unoccupied space within 10 feet of its destination space. |
| **Teleport (Recharge 4–6)** | Blink Dog | The dog teleports up to 40 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. |
| **Teleport (Recharge 5–6)** | Marilith, Mezzoloth | The mezzoloth teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. It can teleport one creature it is grappling to an unoccupied space within 5 feet of its destination space. |
| **Trample** | Elephant, Gorgon, Mammoth | Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 16, one creature within 5 feet that has the Prone condition. Failure: 17 (2d10 + 6) Bludgeoning damage. Success: Half damage. |
| **Trampling Charge (Recharge 5–6)** | Centaur Trooper | The centaur moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space the centaur enters is targeted once by the following effect. Strength Saving Throw: DC 14. Failure: 7 (1d6 + 4) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition. |
| **Tree Stride** | Dryad | If within 5 feet of a Large or bigger tree, the dryad teleports to an unoccupied space within 5 feet of a second Large or bigger tree that is within 60 feet of the previous tree. |
| **Unicorn’s Blessing (3/Day)** | Unicorn | The unicorn touches another creature with its horn and casts Cure Wounds or Lesser Restoration on that creature, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. |
| **Utterance of Pain** | Speaker Devil | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 18, each creature in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the devil. Failure: The target has the Stunned condition until the end of the devil’s next turn. |
| **Utterance of Unmaking** | Speaker Devil | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 18, each creature in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the devil. Failure: 22 (4d10) Force damage. Success: Half damage. |
| **Vanish** | Poltergeist | The poltergeist gives itself the Invisible condition or ends that condition on itself. |
| **Vanish (1)** | Will-o’-Wisp | The wisp and its light have the Invisible condition until the wisp’s Concentration ends on this effect, which ends early immediately after the wisp makes an attack roll or uses Consume Life. |
| **Vow of Revenge (1/Day)** | Revenant | The revenant curses one creature it can see within 30 feet of itself. The revenant knows the distance to and direction of the cursed target, even if it is on a different plane of existence. The curse ends on the target if the revenant uses this Bonus Action on a different creature. |
| **War Cry (Recharge 5–6)** | Frost Giant | The giant or one creature of its choice that can see or hear it gains 16 (2d10 + 5) Temporary Hit Points and has Advantage on attack rolls until the start of the giant’s next turn. |
| **Warp Step** | Dire Worg | The worg teleports, along with a willing creature of its choice within 5 feet of it, up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. |
| **Watery Rush** | Lacedon Ghoul | While underwater, the ghoul moves up to half its Swim Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. |
| **Weird Insight (Recharge 6)** | Nothic | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature the nothic can see within 120 feet. Failure: The nothic magically learns one fact or secret about the target. |
| **Witch Strike** | Arch-hag | Each creature cursed by the hag and within 60 feet of it takes 14 (4d6) Lightning damage. |

| **Counterattack** | Warrior Commander | Trigger: The warrior is hit by an attack roll. Response: The warrior adds 4 to its AC against that attack, possibly causing it to miss. On a miss, the warrior can make one Greatsword or Longbow attack against the attacker. |

Response: The giant reduces the damage it takes from the attack by 11 (1d10 + 6), and if that damage is reduced to 0, the giant can redirect some of the attack’s force. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 17, one creature the giant can see within 60 feet. Failure: 11 (1d10 + 6) Force damage. |

Response: The octopus releases ink that fills a 5-foot Cube centered on itself, and the octopus moves up to its Swim Speed. The Cube is Heavily Obscured for 1 minute or until a strong current or similar effect disperses the ink. |

Response: The noble adds 2 to its AC against that attack, possibly causing it to miss. |

Response: The wearer gains a +5 bonus to AC, including against the triggering attack and possibly causing it to miss, until the start of the guardian’s next turn. |

Response: The ooze splits into two new oozes of its kind (Black Puddings or Ochre Jellies). Each new ooze is one size smaller than the original ooze and acts on its Initiative. The original ooze’s Hit Points are divided evenly between the new oozes (round down). |
| **Spontaneous Barrier (Recharge 4–6)** | Medani Inquisitive | Trigger: The inquisitive or an ally it can see is hit with an attack roll. Response: The inquisitive adds 2 to its or the ally’s AC against that attack, possibly causing it to miss. |

Failure: The attack’s weapon sticks to the kuo-toa’s shield. If the target doesn’t let go of the weapon, the target has the Grappled condition while the weapon is stuck (escape DC 11). While stuck, the weapon can’t be used. The target can take an action to make a DC 11 Strength (Athletics) check, freeing the weapon on a success. |

Each creature within 10 feet of the space the enforcer left must succeed on a DC 13 Constitution saving throw, or its Speed is halved until the start of the enforcer’s next turn. |
| **Tongue Twister** | Arch-hag | The hag casts Counterspell in response to that spell’s trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. If the target fails its saving throw, it is cursed until the end of its next turn. Until the curse ends, the target can’t cast spells with a Verbal component, and when it speaks, it says the opposite of what it means. |
| **Toxic Escape** | Yochlol | Trigger: The yochlol is hit by an attack roll. Response: The yochlol halves the attack’s damage to itself (round down), and it teleports to an unoccupied space it can see within 30 feet of itself. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature within 5 feet of the yochlol’s destination space. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. While Poisoned, it has the Incapacitated condition. |

Response—Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, the triggering creature. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its turn. Success: The target is immune to this devil’s Unnerving Gaze for 24 hours. |

| **Watery Rebuke** | Merfolk Wavebender | Trigger: An enemy the merfolk can see enters a space within 5 feet of the merfolk. Response—Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, the triggering enemy. Failure: 14 (4d6) Cold damage. If the target is Large or smaller, it is pushed up to 30 feet straight away from the merfolk by conjured water. |
| **Whirlwind of Sand** | Mummy Lord | Trigger: The mummy is hit by an attack roll. Response: The mummy adds 2 to its AC against the attack, possibly causing the attack to miss, and the mummy teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. Each creature of its choice that it can see within 5 feet of its destination space has the Blinded condition until the end of the mummy’s next turn. |
| **Word of Stasis (Recharge 5–6)** | Sivis Scribe | Trigger: An enemy within 60 feet of the scribe starts its turn, moves, or makes an attack roll. Response—Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 20-foot-radius Sphere centered on a point the scribe can see within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Incapacitated condition until the start of the scribe’s next turn. While the target is Incapacitated, its Speed is 0 and can’t increase. *(Note: Can also be used as a Bonus Action)*. |
