The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Giggling Magic** | Adult Copper Dragon, Ancient Copper Dragon | Charisma Saving Throw: DC 17, one creature the dragon can see within 90 feet. Failure: 24 (7d6) Psychic damage. Until the end of its next turn, the target rolls 1d6 whenever it makes an ability check or attack roll and subtracts the number rolled from the D20 Test. Failure or Success: The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Glare** | Mummy Lord | The mummy uses Dreadful Glare. The mummy can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Grasping Glob** | Blob of Annihilation | The blob uses Restraining Glob. The blob can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Grave-Dust Flight** | Demilich | The demilich flies up to its Fly Speed, shedding grave dust. Each creature within 5 feet of the demilich as it moves is targeted once by the following effect. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 19. Failure: The target has the Blinded condition until the end of the demilich’s next turn. Failure or Success: The demilich can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Guiding Light** | Adult Bronze Dragon, Adult Gold Dragon, Ancient Bronze Dragon, Ancient Gold Dragon | The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast Guiding Bolt (level 2 version). |
| **Haki (Costs 1 or 2 Actions)** | Apex Bosses, Warlords, Admirals, Emperors | The creature expends 1 or 2 actions to unleash a legendary Haki maneuver—such as emitting a pulse of Conqueror's Haki (forcing nearby creatures to succeed on a Wisdom saving throw or have the Frightened or Incapacitated condition), using Future Sight to predict moves and reposition without provoking Opportunity Attacks, or releasing an advanced Ryuo burst (dealing Force damage that bypasses Temporary HP), as detailed in [Chapter 8: Haki](#chapter-8-haki). |
| **Heightened Awareness (Costs 1 Action)** | Watchers, Inquisitors, Apex Dragons | The monster takes the Search action, or makes a Wisdom (Perception) check to detect hidden creatures. |
| **Infernal Majesty** | Vampire Infernalist | Charisma Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the vampire. Failure: 9 (2d8) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: Half damage only. Failure or Success: The vampire can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Malicious Magic** | Arch-hag | The hag uses Spellcasting to cast Dimension Door or Hypnotic Pattern. The hag can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Mind Invasion** | Adult Green Dragon, Ancient Green Dragon | The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast Mind Spike (level 3 version). |
| **Mind Jolt** | Adult Copper Dragon, Ancient Copper Dragon | The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast Mind Spike (level 4 version). The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Noxious Miasma** | Adult Green Dragon, Ancient Green Dragon | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 20-foot-radius Sphere centered on a point the dragon can see within 90 feet. Failure: 7 (2d6) Poison damage, and the target takes a −2 penalty to AC until the end of its next turn. Failure or Success: The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Psychic Drain** | Aboleth | One creature Charmed by the aboleth that it can see within 30 feet takes 10 (3d6) Psychic damage, and the aboleth regains a number of Hit Points equal to the damage dealt. The aboleth can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Radiant Teleport** | Solar | The solar teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 25, each creature in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the solar at its destination space. Failure: 11 (2d10) Radiant damage. Success: Half damage. |
| **Rumbling Movement** | Elemental Cataclysm | The cataclysm moves up to its Speed, Fly Speed, or Swim Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. Each creature within 5 feet of the cataclysm as it moves is targeted once by the following effect. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 23. Failure: The target has the Prone condition. Failure or Success: The cataclysm can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Scorching Sands** | Adult Brass Dragon, Ancient Brass Dragon | Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 16, one creature the dragon can see within 120 feet. Failure: 27 (6d8) Fire damage, and the target’s Speed is halved until the end of its next turn. Failure or Success: The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Shimmering Shield** | Unicorn | The unicorn targets itself or one creature it can see within 60 feet of itself. The target gains 10 (3d6) Temporary Hit Points, and its AC increases by 2 until the end of the unicorn’s next turn. The unicorn can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Shockwave of Glory** | Empyrean (Celestial), Empyrean (Fiend) | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 23, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the empyrean. Failure: 27 (6d8) Force damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only. Failure or Success: The empyrean can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Sickening Ray** | Dracolich, Dracolich (Gargantuan) | The dracolich uses Spellcasting to cast Ray of Sickness (level 2 version). The dracolich can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Sonic Boom** | Adult Blue Dragon, Ancient Blue Dragon | The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast Shatter. The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Swift Reposition (Costs 1 Action)** | Boss Monsters, Vampires, Sphinxes, Liches | The monster moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. |
| **Tactical Stride (Costs 1 Action)** | Apex Predators, Beast Lords, Cataclysms | The monster moves up to its Speed (or Fly/Swim Speed) without provoking Opportunity Attacks. |
| **Terrifying Presence** | Dracolich, Dracolich (Gargantuan) | Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the dracolich. Failure: 11 (2d10) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. Failure or Success: The dracolich can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Thunderclap** | Adult Bronze Dragon, Ancient Bronze Dragon | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 20-foot-radius Sphere centered on a point the dragon can see within 90 feet. Failure: 10 (3d6) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. |
| **Toxic Ink** | Kraken | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 23, each creature in a 15-foot Emanation originating from the kraken while it is underwater. Failure: The target has the Blinded and Poisoned conditions until the end of the kraken’s next turn. The kraken then moves up to its Speed. Failure or Success: The kraken can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Veil of Shadow** | Shadow Dragon (Huge), Shadow Dragon (Large) | The dragon uses Shadow Stealth, and one creature of its choice that it can see within 10 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Necrotic damage. The dragon can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Weight of Years** | Sphinx of Lore, Sphinx of Valor | Constitution Saving Throw: DC 16, one creature the sphinx can see within 120 feet. Failure: The target gains 1 Exhaustion level. While the target has any Exhaustion levels, it appears 3d10 years older. Failure or Success: The sphinx can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |
| **Wing Beat (Costs 2 Actions)** | Dragons, Celestials, Winged Bosses | Dexterity Saving Throw: DC [8 + Proficiency + Str/Dex modifier], each creature in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the monster. Failure: 11 (2d6 + 4) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Failure or Success: The monster flies up to half its Fly Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. |
| **World-Shaking Movement** | Tarrasque | The tarrasque moves up to its Speed. At the end of this movement, the tarrasque creates an instantaneous shock wave in a 60-foot Emanation originating from itself. Creatures in that area lose Concentration and, if Medium or smaller, have the Prone condition. The tarrasque can’t take this action again until the start of its next turn. |

## Archetypal Monster Templates

One Piece dünyasındaki encounter'lar çoğu zaman tekil creature'lar yerine kendine has kimliği ve savaş doktrini olan belirli gruplardan oluşur. Aşağıda, evrenin en ikonik yedi arketipik grubu için hazırlanmış modüler template'ler yer almaktadır.

Her grup için tek bir temel Monster Sheet hazırlanmıştır. DM'ler encounter'ın seviyesine göre tablodaki köşeli parantezli değerleri (`[AC]`, `[HP]`, `[Attack Bonus]`, `[Save DC]`, `[Attacks]`, `[Hit Damage]`, `[Recharge Damage]`) her template'in altındaki CR Tier tablosundan alarak creature'ı saniyeler içinde masaya hazır hale getirebilir.

> **Tasarım Notu ve Damage Kalibrasyonu (D&D 2024 Standardı):**  
> Template'lerdeki `[Hit Damage]` değeri, creature'ın her bir tekil vuruşunun Hit hasarını temsil eder ve creature'ın ilgili Ability Modifier'ı ile tam uyumludur. `Multiattack` ile yapılan attack'ların toplamı, D&D 2024 standartlarındaki hedef Damage Per Round (DPR) bütçesini tam olarak karşılar. Area of Effect (AoE) veya Recharge eylemleri (`[Recharge Damage]`) ise creature'ın bir round'luk toplam DPR bütçesine göre dengelenmiştir.

### Template 1: Sea Kings

*Bu şablon, temel bir çerçevedir. Karşılaşmanızın konseptine göre Modular Monster Feature Library'den uygun Traits, Actions, Bonus Actions, Reactions ve Legendary Actions ekleyerek bu şablonu dilediğiniz gibi özelleştirebilirsiniz.*

> **Sea King Template**  
> *Gargantuan Monstrosity (Titan), Unaligned*  
> 
> - **Armor Class:** [AC] (Doğal Zırh / Pullu Kalın Deri)  
> - **Hit Points:** [HP]  
> - **Speed:** 20 ft., Swim 60 ft.  
> 
> | STR | DEX | CON | INT | WIS | CHA |
> | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
> | 24 (+7) | 10 (+0) | 22 (+6) | 6 (−2) | 12 (+1) | 8 (−1) |
> 
> - **Saving Throws:** Str +[PB+7], Con +[PB+6]  
> - **Damage Resistances:** Cold, Bludgeoning  
> - **Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 11  
> - **Languages:** Sea King, Telepathy 120 ft. (Yalnızca "Voice of All Things" duyanlar ile)  
> - **Challenge:** [CR] (PB +[PB])  
> 
> **Traits**  
> - **Amphibious.** Sea king hem havayı hem de suyu soluyabilir.  
> - **Siege Monster.** Sea king nesnelere, yapılara ve gemilere saldırdığında onların Damage Threshold değerini tamamen yok sayar.  
> 
> **Actions**  
> - **Multiattack.** Sea king [Attacks] saldırı yapar: biri Bite ve [Attacks-1] adedi Constrict veya Tail Slam ile.  
> - **Bite.** Melee Attack Roll: +[Attack Bonus], reach 15 ft. Hit: [Hit Damage] Piercing damage.  
> - **Constrict / Tail Slam.** Melee Attack Roll: +[Attack Bonus], reach 20 ft. Hit: [Hit Damage] Bludgeoning damage. Eğer hedef Large veya daha küçük bir creature ise Grappled condition'ına girer (escape DC [Save DC]).  
> - **Tidal Surge (Recharge 5–6).** Sea king devasa bir dalga yaratarak çırpınır. Strength Saving Throw: DC [Save DC], 60-foot Cone içindeki her creature ve gemi. Failure: [Recharge Damage] Bludgeoning damage alır, 30 feet uzağa itilir ve Prone condition'ına girer. Success: Half damage alır ve itilmez.

>

> 
> | STR | DEX | CON | INT | WIS | CHA |
> | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
> | 18 (+4) | 16 (+3) | 16 (+3) | 8 (−1) | 10 (+0) | 12 (+1) |
> 
> - **Saving Throws:** Str +[PB+4], Con +[PB+3]  
> - **Skills:** Athletics +[PB+4], Perception +[PB+0]  
> - **Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft., Passive Perception 12  
> - **Languages:** Common  
> - **Challenge:** [CR] (PB +[PB])  
> 
> **Traits**  
> - **Grotesque Hybrid Adaptation.** Aşağıdaki özelliklerden 1 tanesini seçin veya zar atın:  
>   1. *Scorpion Tail:* Reach 10 ft. kazanır ve stinger saldırısı Poison damage verir.  
>   2. *Lion Belly:* Kokuya dayalı Perception check'lerinde Advantage kazanır ve ek Bite saldırısı elde eder.  
>   3. *Bat Ears:* Blindsight 60 ft. ve Fly speed 30 ft. kazanır.  
>   4. *Armored Beetle Carapace:* AC'sine +2 eklenir.  
> - **Reckless Pounce.** Eğer Gifter bir creature'a doğru en az 20 feet düz hareket edip aynı turn'de bir melee attack ile vurursa, hedef DC [Save DC] Strength saving throw yapmalıdır; başarısızlık halinde Prone condition'ına girer.  
> 
> **Actions**  
> - **Multiattack.** Gifter [Attacks] saldırı yapar: Silahı ve Beast Mutasyonu (Bite, Claw, Horn veya Stinger) ile.  
> - **Brutal Weapon Strike.** Melee Attack Roll: +[Attack Bonus], reach 5 ft. Hit: [Hit Damage] Slashing veya Bludgeoning damage.  
> - **Mutated Beast Strike.** Melee Attack Roll: +[Attack Bonus], reach 5–10 ft. Hit: [Hit Damage] Piercing damage artı [Bonus Poison Damage] Poison veya Acid damage.

> **Actions**

> - **Mizudeppo (Water Shot).** Ranged Attack Roll: +[Attack Bonus], range 60/180 ft. Hit: [Hit Damage] Piercing damage.  
> - **Vagabond Drill (Recharge 5–6).** Fish-man yüksek basınçlı bir su mızrağı şok dalgası fırlatır. Strength Saving Throw: DC [Save DC], 40-foot-long, 5-foot-wide Line içindeki her creature. Failure: [Recharge Damage] Bludgeoning damage alır ve Prone condition'ına girer. Success: Half damage alır ve Prone olmaz.

## Unique and Mythological Creatures

One Piece evreninde standart gruplara sığmayan, tarihe yön veren veya tekil bulunan efsanevi mitolojik varlıklar ve gizli tehditler mevcuttur. Aşağıdaki fihrist, bu kadim varlıkların isimlerini ve ait oldukları ark/kategorileri listeler:

### Titanic and Mythological Sea Creatures

- **Naitamie-Norida Elephant (Zunesha):** Sırtında Mokomo Dukedom (Minks) medeniyetini taşıyan, bacakları okyanus tabanına basan ve 800 yıldır cezalı olarak yürüyen 35.000 metre boyundaki kadim dünya devi fil.
- **Island Eater:** Little Garden kıyılarında beliren, dışkıları ada büyüklüğünde olan ve devasa korsan gemilerini tek lokmada yutan tarih öncesi goldfish canavarı.
- **Lord of the Coast:** Foosha Village kıyılarında yaşayan, Shanks'ın sol kolunu alan ve Luffy'nin deniz yolculuğuna başlarken tek bir Gomu Gomu no Pistol ile yendiği ikonik deniz canavarı.
- **Surume (Kraken):** Balıkadam Adası rotasındaki 10.000 metre derinlikteki sualtı volkanik çukurlarında yaşayan, Luffy tarafından evcilleştirilen dev ahtapot kralı.
- **Wadatsumi:** Flying Dutchman gemisini okyanus derinliklerinde çeken, Sanji ve Jinbe ile yüzleşen devasa Tiger Blowfish balıkadam devi.
- **Laboon (Island Whale):** Reverse Mountain girişinde Rumbar Pirates ve Brook'u 50 yıldır bekleyen, kafası Red Line kayalıklarına vurulmaktan yara izleriyle dolu ada boyutundaki sadık balina.
- **Nola (Skypiea Giant God Snake):** 400 yıl önce Shandora'da "Kashigami" olarak tapınılan, Karagara ve Noland'ın dostu olan ve Skypiea Upper Yard ormanlarında yaşayan dev tanrı yılanı.
- **Mecha-Sea Kings:** Egghead adasını koruyan, Dr. Vegapunk tarafından sibernetik zırh, sonar ve lazer sistemleriyle donatılmış silahlı dev deniz canavarları.

### World Government and Ancient Hidden Threats

- **Gorosei Kadim Yōkai Formları (Five Elders):** World Government'ın en tepesindeki beş kadim liderin sahip olduğu, konvansiyonel saldırılarla yaralanamayan (sonsuz yenilenme) ve siyah yıldırım auraları yayan iblis formları:
  - *Saint Jaygarcia Saturn (Gyūki):* Zehirli bacaklı, alevli boynuzlu devasa Ushi-Oni (Öküz-Örümcek).
  - *Saint Marcus Mars (Itsumade):* Gökyüzünü ateşe veren devasa kuş-sürüngen canavarı.
  - *Saint Topman Warcury (Fengxi):* Çelikten sert zırhlı derisi olan, kılıçları ve yumrukları kıran titanik yaban domuzu.
  - *Saint Ethanbaron V. Nusjuro (Bakotsu):* Buzlu kılıç darbeleriyle Pacifistaları ikiye bölen iskelet at (Kitsune-Kirin hibriti).
  - *Saint Shepherd Ju Peter (Sandworm):* Yer altından çıkıp binaları ve insanları yutan devasa çöl kurdu.
- **Pacifista PX-0 (Bartholomew Kuma):** Eski Devrim Ordusu komutanı ve Shichibukai Kuma'nın bilincinin silinmesiyle tamamlanan, orijinal Nikyu Nikyu no Mi meyvesine ve sibernetik silahlara sahip ilk prototip.
- **Pacifista Mark III:** Egghead adasında konuşlandırılmış, Bubble Shield (Deniz Taşı baloncuk kalkanı) teknolojisi ve gelişmiş lazer donanımlı yeni nesil savunma Pacifistaları.

### Legendary Monsters and Ancient Calamities

- **Sanjuan Wolf (Colossal Battleship):** Blackbeard Pirates devi; Şeytan Meyvesi sayesinde boyutu 180 metreye ulaşan ve doğrudan okyanus tabanında yürüyebilen yaşayan dev.
- **Oars & Little Oars Jr. (Continent-Puller Giants):** Normal devlerin katbekat büyüklüğünde olan, tarihte adaları yerinden oynatarak krallıklar kurduğu rivayet edilen efsanevi boynuzlu dev ırkı.
- **Thriller Bark Cerberus:** Gecko Moria ve Dr. Hogback tarafından mezarlıklardan toplanan parçalarla üretilmiş üç başlı zombi bekçi köpeği (iki köpek kafası ve bir tilki kafası).
- **Punk Hazard Dragons (Dragon Number Thirteen & Small Dragon):** Dr. Vegapunk tarafından Punk Hazard laboratuvarlarında genetik mühendislikle üretilmiş, sert pullara ve alev püskürtme kabiliyetine sahip yapay ejderhalar.

## NotebookLM Monster Assistant

Dungeon Master'lar, campaign esnasında dinamik ve özelleştirilmiş One Piece canavarları, adaya özgü yırtıcı faunalar ve özgün korsan/marine mürettebatları oluşturmak için projenin özel eğitilmiş yapay zeka asistanından faydalanabilirler:

- **Resmi Asistan Bağlantısı:** [One Piece D&D 2024: NotebookLM Knowledge Assistant](https://notebook.google.com/notebook/e0600e4e-90dd-4662-88ff-cb7a8dfc6ed7)

*Monster sheet üretme ve sorularınızı yanıtlama gibi birçok işinize yarar. Sorularınızı doğru sorduğunuzdan ve yeterince ayrıntı verdiğinizden emin olun.*
